All stem from a single root: codeblocks in spec docs that purport to be "shipped excerpts" contained editorial content that was NOT present in the shipped file. The durable structural fix: shipped-excerpt codeblocks must be BYTE-FOR-BYTE verbatim — no editorial additions, no omissions, no paraphrase. If commentary is needed, it belongs in prose surrounding the codeblock, clearly separated. This principle structurally eliminates the codeblock-fidelity drift class: once all codeblocks are verbatim, any future drift is immediately detectable by diff, not by interpretation. Codify as dimension (g) in S-7.02 spec-text-drift sweep protocol: (g) verbatim-codeblock: for every codeblock claiming to excerpt a shipped file, diff against the actual shipped file byte-for-byte; any editorial insertion or omission is a finding. **CODE UNCHANGED — PR HEAD c210eb1f UNCHANGED.
